## district_ark_city_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_ark_city_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一大块划归居住、商业及社区功能的舰船区段。它是我们迁徙文明涌动的核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

Vast sections of the ship given over to housing, commerce, and community. The living heart of a civilization in motion.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:18`

## job_ark_waystation_trader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_ark_waystation_trader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:31`

### English

The remote management of stations is a thankless task of endless logistic needs, but someone must handle it.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:35`

### English

## zone_ark_missile_silos_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_missile_silos_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

专业的维护组会对导弹舱进行定期维护保养，确保其状态良好随时待发，他们以纯粹的能源消耗为代价换取了先发制人的打击能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

Dedicated maintenance crews keep the missile bays primed and ready, trading raw energy output for strike-first firepower.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:44`

## zone_ark_ammunition_lines

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_ammunition_lines&lang=en)

### 中文

弹药生产线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Ammunition Lines

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:45`

## zone_ark_ammunition_lines_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_ammunition_lines_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

全天候生产动能弹药，确保我们的巨炮永不哑火。每一件装填器、每一个弹壳、每一份推进剂的用量都经过精确计算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

Round-the-clock fabrication of kinetic rounds ensures the guns are never silent. Every loader, every casing, every gram of propellant is accounted for.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:46`

## zone_ark_laser_capacitors

## zone_ark_laser_capacitors_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_laser_capacitors_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一排排高容量电容直接向激光阵列灌输能量，在发射器散热能力的极限维持开火速率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:44`

### English

Banks of high-cycle capacitors feed energy directly to the beam arrays, sustaining fire rates that would otherwise overheat the emitters.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:48`

## district_ark_engine_bypass_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_ark_engine_bypass_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

引擎旁路系统摒弃了传统的冷却方式，转而将多余能量直接用于驱动舰船引擎，但这一做法的代价是能量币的产量随之降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Instead of relying on traditional cooling methods, the Engine Bypass directly reuses excess energy to fuel the ship's engines, at the cost of reduced energy credit production.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:62`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:82`

## zone_ark_harvest_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_harvest_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在方舟上划分出专用空间，用于对采集到的资源进行分类与初筛，可以利用重力场下的操作优势与高效机械设备，从而提高资源产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Reserving dedicated arkship space for the collection and triage of harvested resources offers a higher yield thanks to the opportunity of in-gravity operations and the use of more streamlined machinery.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:83`

### 中文

## job_ark_harvester_gestalt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_ark_harvester_gestalt_desc&lang=en)

宇航员配备的采收子个体，是专为执行太空行走任务而设计的，其主要职责是离开方舟，前往舱外采集轨道上的矿产资源。

## zone_ark_military_eco_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_military_eco_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们部分铸造厂改造为专门生产标准化装甲板的工厂，即可为我们提供随时可用的烧蚀防护备用件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:122`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_ark_shipwright_desc&lang=en)

### English

### English

Teeming corridors and communal chambers fill every level of this Arkship, humming with collective purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:196`

## MESSAGE_ENEMY_ARKSHIP_DESTROYED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_ENEMY_ARKSHIP_DESTROYED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

位于§Y[FromFrom.solar_system.GetName]§!恒星系的方舟$SHIP|Y$已被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

## concept_nomadic_empire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_nomadic_empire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£nomad£ §H$concept_nomadic_empires$§!是一种运作在可移动的£arkship£ ['concept_arkships']之上，而非扎根于固定的£colony£ ['concept_colonies']的文明。他们无法宣称£system£ ['concept_systems']或者殖民£planet£ ['concept_planets']。\n\n§H游牧帝国§!能修建£waystation£ ['concept_waystations']来收集资源，也可以接受£planet£ ['concept_settled_empires']发来的£contract£ ['concept_contracts']。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

£nomad£ §H$concept_nomadic_empires$§! are civilizations that operate from mobile £arkship£ ['concept_arkships'] rather than stationary £colony£ ['concept_colonies']. They do not claim £system£ ['concept_systems'] or colonize £planet£ ['concept_planets'].\n\n§HNomadic Empires§! can build £waystation£ ['concept_waystations'] to collect resources, and can accept £contract£ ['concept_contracts'] from £planet£ ['concept_settled_empires'].

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:343`

## concept_operational_reserves_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_operational_reserves_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires']的§H$concept_operational_reserves$§!是£minerals£ ['concept_minerals']与£energy£ ['concept_energy']的综合展现。\n\n£minerals£ §H$concept_minerals$§!和£energy£§H$concept_energy$§!仍有收入与支出，只是会以['concept_situation']的形式呈现。\n\n每一点§H$concept_operational_reserves$§!等价于一点£minerals£ §H矿物§!或£energy£ §H能量币§!。\n\n$nomad_max_reserves_breakdown$\n\n局势的各个§H阶段§!将对帝国£arkship£ ['concept_arkships']的许多方面产生影响，你也可以选择不同§H应对方案§!来节约资源或者燃烧资源以提高生产力。\n\n你可以从£market£ ['concept_market']中购买£minerals£ §H$concept_minerals$§!和£energy£ §H$concept_energy$§!，但无法售卖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

The §H$concept_operational_reserves$§! for £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires'] are a combined view of £minerals£['concept_minerals'] and £energy£ ['concept_energy'].\n\nThere is still income and upkeep of £minerals£ §H$concept_minerals$§! and £energy£ §H$concept_energy$§!, but managed as a ['concept_situation'].\n\nOne point of §H$concept_operational_reserves$§! is equal to one £minerals£ §HMineral§! or one £energy£ §HEnergy Credit§!.\n\n$nomad_max_reserves_breakdown$\n\nThe §HStages§! of the situation affect the empire's £arkship£ ['concept_arkships'] in different ways, and §HApproaches§! can be selected to conserve resources or burn through them for higher productivity.\n\n£minerals£ §H$concept_minerals$§! and £energy£ §H$concept_energy$§! can be bought on the £market£ ['concept_market'], but not sold.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:353`

## concept_settled_empire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_settled_empire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£planet£ §H$concept_settled_empires$§!是指以£planet£ ['concept_planets']上固定的£colony£['concept_colonies']为根基进行统治的文明，他们能够宣称£system£ ['concept_systems']并利用['concept_starbases']进行防御。\n\n对于£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires']而言，§H定居帝国§!是潜在的£contract£ ['concept_contracts']与['concept_diplomatic_pacts']来源，不过当游牧舰队跨越定居帝国边境时也可能会引发外交摩擦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

£planet£ §H$concept_settled_empires$§! are civilizations that govern from stationary £colony£ ['concept_colonies'] on £planet£ ['concept_planets'], claiming £system£ ['concept_systems'] and defending them with ['concept_starbases'].\n\nFrom the perspective of £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires'], §HSettled Empires§! are potential partners for £contract£ ['concept_contracts'] and ['concept_diplomatic_pacts'], though diplomatic friction may arise when nomadic fleets cross their borders.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:360`

## concept_contract_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_contract_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£contract£ §H$concept_contracts$§!既有由£planet£ ['concept_settled_empires']发布给£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires']的任务，也有§H$concept_nomadic_empire$§!自身内部提出的任务。\n\n这些任务囊括了从突袭宿敌到运送外交官等一系列操作，通常只能由£arkship£ ['concept_arkships']来完成。\n\n§H合约§!在£interface£ ['concept_situation_log_view']中的$situation_log_view_tab_situation_log$选项卡中列出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

£contract£ §H$concept_contracts$§! are tasks given to £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires'] by £planet£ ['concept_settled_empires'], or internal tasks from parts of the §H$concept_nomadic_empire$§! itself.\n\nThese tasks range from raiding rivals to transporting diplomats, and can typically only be completed by £arkship£ ['concept_arkships'].\n\n§HContracts§! are listed in the $situation_log_view_tab_situation_log$ tab of the £interface£ ['concept_situation_log_view'].

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:366`

## concept_waystation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_waystation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£waystation£ §H$concept_waystations$§!是一种空间站，由£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires']所建造，能够从天体收集资源，并建立星际贸易航路，也被称为£wayline£ ['concept_waylines']。\n\n§H$concept_waystations$§!收集的资源被存储在§H驿站库存§!之中，等待着£construction_ship£ ['concept_logistic_ship']或者£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']前来提取。\n\n即使没有§H恒星系所有者§!的允许，也可以在£system£ ['concept_systems']中建造§H$concept_waystations$§!，不过这会给恒星系所有者的外交带来负面影响，给予他们§H$war_goal_wg_expel_nomads$§!战争目标。签署['concept_wayline_pact']则可以移除这些负面惩罚。$DOUBLE_NEW_LINE$帝国所能维持的§H$concept_waystations$§!数量由['concept_starbase_capacity']所限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

£waystation£ §H$concept_waystations$§! are space stations, built by £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires'], that can collect celestial resources and form interstellar trade routes, known as £wayline£ ['concept_waylines'].\n\nResources collected by §H$concept_waystations$§! are held in the §HWaystation Stockpile§! until visited by a £construction_ship£ ['concept_logistic_ship'] or £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'].\n\n§H$concept_waystations$§! can be constructed in £system£ ['concept_systems'] without permission from the §HSystem Owner§!, which incurs Diplomatic Penalties with the System Owner, granting them the §H$war_goal_wg_expel_nomads$§! War Goal. Signing a ['concept_wayline_pact'] will remove these penalties. $DOUBLE_NEW_LINE$The number of §H$concept_waystations$§! an empire can support is limited by its ['concept_starbase_capacity'].

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:372`

## concept_wayline_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_wayline_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£wayline£ §H$concept_waylines$§!是由相邻£system£ ['concept_systems']中的£waystation£ ['concept_waystations']建立的星际贸易航线。\n\n§H驿站模块§!会提供沿§H$concept_wayline$§!传播的效果与修正，这些效果也会通过与§H恒星系所有者§!签订的['concept_wayline_pacts']进行共享。例如，§H$sm_waystation_logistics_hub$§!能够收集整条£waystation£ §H$concept_wayline$§!沿线的所有§H$concept_waystations$§!存储的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

£wayline£ §H$concept_waylines$§! are interstellar trade routes formed by £waystation£ ['concept_waystations'] in neighboring £system£ ['concept_systems'].\n\n§HWaystation Modules§! provide effects and modifiers that are propagated along the §H$concept_wayline$§! and shared by ['concept_wayline_pacts'] with the §HSystem Owner§!. For example, the §H$sm_waystation_logistics_hub$§! allows for the collection of stockpiled resources from all £waystation£ §H$concept_waystations$§! along an entire §H$concept_wayline$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:378`

## concept_wayline_pact_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_wayline_pact_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$concept_wayline_pacts$§!是一种由£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empire']与另一个帝国所签订的['concept_diplomatic_pacts']。这些条约能让双方帝国从沿§H驿道§!传播的£wayline£ ['concept_wayline']§H修正§!中获益。\n\n此外，如果条约是在§H$concept_nomadic_empire$§!与£planet£ ['concept_settled_empire']之间签订的，那么§H驿道§!所产生的外交惩罚将消失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

§H$concept_wayline_pacts$§! are ['concept_diplomatic_pacts'] signed between a £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empire'] and another empire. These agreements allow both Empires to benefit from the £wayline£ ['concept_wayline'] §HModifiers§! propagated along the §HWayline§!.\n\nAdditionally, when signed between a §H$concept_nomadic_empire$§! and a £planet£ ['concept_settled_empire'], any Diplomatic Penalties caused by the §HWayline§! are ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:384`

## concept_logistic_ship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_logistic_ship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£construction_ship£§H$concept_logistic_ship$§!可以从天体中收获资源并运输到$arkship_cap_plural$。$tt_use$—从行星、卫星、小行星中['scripted_action:logistic_harvest_resources']$NEW_LINE$—在£system£['concept_systems']中建造['concept_waystations']以扩展£wayline£ ['concept_waylines']$NEW_LINE$—建造£waystation£ ['concept_megastructures']$NEW_LINE$—从£waystation£ §H$concept_waystations$§!中收集资源$NEW_LINE$—无法像其他§H方舟舰§!一样建造新的£arkship£ ['concept_arkships']$NEW_LINE$—研究特定['concept_special_projects']$tt_gain$—在£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']中建造§H$concept_logistic_ships$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

A £construction_ship£ §H$concept_logistic_ship$§! can harvest resources from celestial bodies and transport them to our $arkship_cap_plural$.$tt_use$- ['scripted_action:logistic_harvest_resources'] from Planets, Moons and Asteroids$NEW_LINE$- Build £waystation£ ['concept_waystations'] in £system£ ['concept_systems'] to expand our £wayline£ ['concept_waylines']$NEW_LINE$- Build ['concept_megastructures'] $NEW_LINE$- Collect resources from £waystation£ §H$concept_waystations$§! $NEW_LINE$- Cannot build new £arkship£ ['concept_arkships'], which only other §HArkships§! can do $NEW_LINE$- Research certain ['concept_special_projects']$tt_gain$- Build §H$concept_logistic_ships$§! in an £arkship£ ['concept_arkship']

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:390`

## concept_arkship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_arkship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£arkship£ §H$concept_arkships$§!是载着£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires']穿越太空虚空的巨舰，可同时作为£colony£ ['concept_colony']、['concept_starbase']和['concept_military_ship']运作。$DOUBLE_NEW_LINE$—作为§H殖民地§!，其上包含£district£ ['concept_districts']、£zone£ ['concept_district_specializations']、£building£ ['concept_buildings']和£pop£ ['concept_pops']。$NEW_LINE$—作为§H恒星基地§!，它们可充当['concept_shipyards']，并可使用§H方舟舰模块§!和§H系统§!进行定制。$NEW_LINE$—§H$concept_arkships$§!的所有§H殖民地§!与§H恒星基地§!建造共享同一个建造队列。$NEW_LINE$—作为§H军用舰船§!，§H$concept_arkships$§!拥有多个机库与点防御，以及可观的装甲和护盾。$NEW_LINE$—§H$concept_arkships$§!可从£construction_ship£ ['concept_logistic_ships']和£waystation£ ['concept_waystations']处收集囤积的资源。$NEW_LINE$—§H$concept_arkships$§!还可执行更多战略行动，包括：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_survey']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['concept_megastructure', $BUILD_MEGASTRUCTURE_ORDER$]$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—['scripted_action:arkship_harvest_resources']$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—§H方舟舰模块§!可解锁更先进的行动。$NEW_LINE$—研究£engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_construction$§!后，其他§H$concept_arkships$§!可将新的§H$concept_arkships$§!作为['concept_megastructures']建造。$NEW_LINE$—研究£engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_tier_2$§!与£engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_tier_3$§!后，§H$concept_arkships$§!可进行升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

£arkship£ §H$concept_arkships$§! are colossal vessels that carry £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empires'] through the void of space, functioning at once as a £colony£ ['concept_colony'], ['concept_starbase'], and ['concept_military_ship']. $DOUBLE_NEW_LINE$- As §HColonies§!, they contain £district£ ['concept_districts'], £zone£ ['concept_district_specializations'], £building£ ['concept_buildings'] and £pop£ ['concept_pops']. $NEW_LINE$- As §HStarbases§!, they act as ['concept_shipyards'] and can be customized with §HArkship Modules§! and §HSystems§!. $NEW_LINE$- §H$concept_arkships$§! have a shared build queue for all §HColony§! and §HStarbase§! construction. $NEW_LINE$- As §HMilitary Ships§!, §H$concept_arkships$§! boast multiple hangar bays and point-defenses, and significant armor and shields. $NEW_LINE$- §H$concept_arkships$§! can collect stockpiled resources from £construction_ship£ ['concept_logistic_ships'] and £waystation£ ['concept_waystations']. $NEW_LINE$- §H$concept_arkships$§! can perform additional strategic actions, including: $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_survey'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['concept_megastructure', $BUILD_MEGASTRUCTURE_ORDER$] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- ['scripted_action:arkship_harvest_resources'] $TABBED_NEW_LINE$- §HArkship Modules§! unlock more advanced actions. $NEW_LINE$- New §H$concept_arkships$§! can be constructed as ['concept_megastructures'] by other §H$concept_arkships$§! after researching £engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_construction$§!. $NEW_LINE$- §H$concept_arkships$§! can be upgraded after researching £engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_tier_2$§! and £engineering_research£ §Y$tech_arkship_tier_3$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:396`

## concept_arkship_settle_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_arkship_settle_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£settle£ §H$concept_arkship_settle$§!是指让£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']永久停驻在一颗星球上，结束游牧航行的行为。\n\n需要完成['tradition_category:tradition_adaptability']或['tradition_category:tradition_versatility']传统树。\n\n要进行§H$concept_arkship_settle$§!，需要选择一艘§H方舟舰§!，右键点击一颗未殖民['concept_habitable_world']或在£interface£ ['concept_colony_view']点击§H殖民§!按钮。\n\n拥有更多§H方舟§!的帝国可以选择继续作为游牧帝国，并将新殖民的£planet£ ['concept_settled_empire']转变为一个['concept_subject']。\n\n无主£system£ ['concept_systems']内的£waystation£ ['concept_waystations']将会转化为['concept_outposts']。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

To £settle£ §H$concept_arkship_settle$§! is when a £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'] permanently settles on a world, ending its nomadic voyage.\n\nThis requires that either the ['tradition_category:tradition_adaptability'] or ['tradition_category:tradition_versatility'] tradition tree has been completed.\n\nTo §H$concept_arkship_settle$§!, either select an §HArkship§! and right-click an uncolonized ['concept_habitable_world'] or click the §HColonize§! button in the £interface£ ['concept_colony_view'].\n\nAn empire with additional §HArkships§! can choose to let them continue as nomads and turn the newly £planet£ ['concept_settled_empire'] into a ['concept_subject'].\n\n£waystation£ ['concept_waystations'] in unowned £system£ ['concept_systems'] are converted into ['concept_outposts'].

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:404`

## concept_arkship_embark_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_arkship_embark_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£embark£ §H$concept_arkship_embark$§!是指让£planet£ ['concept_settled_empire']离开['concept_habitable_world']，登上一艘£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']变为一个£nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empire']的行为。\n\n这需要完成['tradition_category:tradition_adaptability']或['tradition_category:tradition_versatility']传统树。\n\n要进行§H登船§!，在一个£colony£ ['concept_colony']打开['concept_decisions']界面并选择§H$decision_nomad_embarkation$§!。\n\n如果帝国拥有复数['concept_colonies']，可以选择将余下部分转变为一个['concept_subject']，或是将新形成的§H游牧帝国§!释放为一个§H$concept_subject$§!。\n\n拥有的所有['concept_starbases']转化为£waystation£ ['concept_waystations']，而启程的行星变为§H$stripped_planet$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

To £embark£ §H$concept_arkship_embark$§! is when a £planet£ ['concept_settled_empire'] leaves their ['concept_habitable_world'] and boards an £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'] to become a £nomad£ ['concept_nomadic_empire'].\n\nThis requires that either the ['tradition_category:tradition_adaptability'] or ['tradition_category:tradition_versatility'] tradition tree has been completed.\n\nTo §HEmbark§!, open the ['concept_decisions'] panel on a £colony£ ['concept_colony'] and select §H$decision_nomad_embarkation$§!.\n\nIf an empire has multiple ['concept_colonies'] they can choose to leave them behind as a ['concept_subject'], or instead release the new §HNomadic Empire§! as a §H$concept_subject$§!.\n\nAll owned ['concept_starbases'] are converted into £waystation£ ['concept_waystations'], and the embarked planet becomes a §H$stripped_planet$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:412`

## nomad_max_reserves_breakdown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomad_max_reserves_breakdown&lang=en)

### 中文

§H运行储备容量§!的基础值为§G$@nomad_reserves_base|0$§!。\n我们每拥有一艘§H方舟舰§!可以提升：\n— I级：§G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_1|0$§!\n— II级：§G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_2|0$§!\n— III级：§G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_3|0$§!\n§H民用方舟§!的每一等级提供更多§G$@nomad_reserves_civilian_per_tier_1|0$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

§HOperational Reserve Capacity§! has a base value of §G$@nomad_reserves_base|0$§!.\nEach §HArkship§! we own increases it by:\n- Tier I: §G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_1|0$§!\n- Tier II: §G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_2|0$§!\n- Tier III: §G$@nomad_reserves_per_tier_3|0$§!\n§HCivilian Arkships§! grant an additional §G$@nomad_reserves_civilian_per_tier_1|0$§! per Tier.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:495`

## situation_nomad_economy_stringent_budget_desc

### 中文

我们整个舰队正在勒紧裤腰带过日子。维护费用的降低减少了我们的能量和矿物成本，从而减缓了对运行储备的消耗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

We are tightening expenditures across the fleet. Reduced upkeep lowers our Energy and Mineral costs, slowing the drain on our Operational Reserves.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:501`

## situation_nomad_economy_full_spending_desc

### 中文

### 中文

好奇的天性深埋于蜂巢的DNA之中。它追寻着一颗又一颗陌生的星辰，沉醉于繁星之中。

## civic_machine_star_seekers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_star_seekers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这台机械智能的明确目的就是穿梭于虚空之中，记录一切事物。不要遗漏任何一颗星星，也不要遗漏任何一个生物。日志必须完整且实时更新。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

It is this Machine Intelligence's explicit purpose to traverse the void and record all things. Leave no star unsighted and no creature unlisted. The Journal must be complete and up to date.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:544`

## sm_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor&lang=en)

### 中文

以太相锚定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Aetherophasic Anchor

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:742`

## sm_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此设施将我们漂泊的舰队扎根于宇宙中的一个固定点位。借助锚定于此的驿站，我们指定此恒星系为§H$crisis_sphere_0$§!工程的天锚。我们在星海间收割的能量将汇聚于此，撕裂两界之间的屏障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

This installation tethers our mobile fleet to a fixed point in the cosmos. By anchoring our Waystation here, we designate this system as the focal point for the §H$crisis_sphere_0$§! construction. The energies we harvest across the stars will converge here, tearing open the barrier between worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:743`

## requires_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arequires_waystation_aetherophasic_anchor&lang=en)

### 中文

游牧帝国必须选定§H以太相锚定§!驿站模块的位置才能启动最终阶段。该驿站模块所处的恒星系将承载§H$crisis_sphere_0$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Nomadic empires must designate an §HAetherophasic Anchor§! Waystation module before initiating the final stage. Build this module on a Waystation to choose which system will host the §H$crisis_sphere_0$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:744`

## NOMAD_SETTLING_IN_PROGRESS_FAIL

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANOMAD_SETTLING_IN_PROGRESS_FAIL&lang=en)

### 中文

有一艘§H方舟舰§!目前正在向该恒星系§Y定居§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

An §HArkship§! is currently §YSettling§! in this system.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:747`

## operation_seize_waystation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_seize_waystation&lang=en)

### 中文

夺取驿站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Seize Waystation

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:750`

## operation_seize_waystation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_seize_waystation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

游牧舰队的力量来源于支撑它的驿站网络。控制驿站即可有效限制其行动能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

A nomadic fleet is only as strong as the waystation network that sustains it. Seize the stations, cripple their reach.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:751`

## operation_seize_waystation_on_success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_seize_waystation_on_success&lang=en)

### 中文

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\n劫掠一座游牧驿站，窃取其中储备的资源并摧毁一个模块。\n\n向该行动指派一名§Y线人§!即有概率夺取驿站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

$IF_SUCCESSFUL|E$\nRaid a nomadic waystation, stealing its stockpiled resources and stripping a module.\n\nAssigning an §YAsset§! to this operation might let us destroy or take over the station.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:752`

## operation_seize_waystation_no_targets

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoperation_seize_waystation_no_targets&lang=en)

### 中文

目标帝国在探测范围内没有运转中的驿站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

The target empire operates no waystations within sensor range.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:753`

## nomads.8010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

接近目标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Approaching the Target

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:754`

## nomads.8010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我方特工已经开始接近目标驿站，并绘制了安全模式图，确定了进入位置。我们已准备好推进至下一阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

Our operatives have made their initial approach to the target waystation. Security patterns have been mapped and entry vectors identified. We are ready to move to the next phase.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:755`

## nomads.8015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

系统已渗透

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

Systems Compromised

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:756`

## nomads.8015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的特工现已渗透进驿站的船员、访客与系统之中。关键安全中继器已被禁用，内部通讯正通过我们自己的过滤器转发。这座空间站，已经是我们的囊中之物了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

Our agents are now embedded within the waystation's crew, visitors and systems. Key security relays have been disabled and communications are being routed through our own filters. The station's fate is in our hands.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:757`

## nomads.8020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抢夺驿站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

Waystation Heist

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:758`

## nomads.8020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的情报网络已经确定了一个目标驿站。特工已经渗透完成，准备就绪。增援部队随时待命，准备登上空间站。他们将按我们的指令发起突袭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

Our intelligence network has pinpointed a target waystation. Our operatives are embedded and ready. Reinforcements are standing by to board the station. They will strike at our command.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:759`

## nomads.8025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

控制驿站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Waystation Seized

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:760`

## nomads.8025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我方特工已消灭了§Y[target_waystation.GetName]§!上的安全部队，我们正在转移其资源。待行动完成后，他们将……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Our operatives have neutralized the security forces aboard §Y[target_waystation.GetName]§!, and we are in the process of appropriating their resources. Once they are done, they will...

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:761`

## nomads.8025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

...摧毁空间站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

... destroy the station.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:762`

§R[target_waystation.GetName]§!被摧毁。

### English

§R[target_waystation.GetName]§! is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:763`

## nomads.8025.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8025.b&lang=en)

...拿走物资。

### English

... extract.

... take full control.

## nomads.8030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

驿站被渗透

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Waystation Compromised

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:766`

## nomads.8030.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[target_waystation.GetName]§!遭到了渗透！入侵者以武力强行夺取了空间站的控制权，并洗劫了库存的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

The §Y[target_waystation.GetName]§! was infiltrated! The intruders used force to seize control of the station and appropriate its stockpiles.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:767`

## nomads.8030.detected

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.detected&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管袭击者采取了种种预防措施以隐匿身份，但我们还是查明他们隶属于 §Y[From.GetName]§!。他们必须要为此付出代价！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Despite their precautions to stay anonymous, we have identified the attackers as belonging to the §Y[From.GetName]§!. They will pay for this!

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:768`

## nomads.8030.anonymous

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.anonymous&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们尽了最大努力，但还是无法确认袭击者的真实身份。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Despite our best efforts, we could not manage to identify the attackers.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:769`

## nomads.8030.destroyed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.destroyed&lang=en)

### 中文

炸药引爆后，空间站被破坏，关键系统被摧毁，空间站的完整性受到损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

The station was then scuttled after explosive charges detonated, taking down critical systems and compromising the station's integrity.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:770`

## nomads.8030.looted

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.looted&lang=en)

### 中文

空间站局部在战斗中受损，但在部分敌人先行带着战利品离开后，我们的安全部队设法重新夺回了空间站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

Part of the station was damaged during the fighting, but our security forces managed to regain control after part of the enemies left with their loot.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:771`

## nomads.8030.detected_destroyed.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.detected_destroyed.desc&lang=en)

### English

$nomads.8030.intro$ $nomads.8030.destroyed$\n\n$nomads.8030.detected$

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:772`

## nomads.8030.detected_looted.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.detected_looted.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

$nomads.8030.intro$ $nomads.8030.looted$\n\n$nomads.8030.detected$

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:773`

## nomads.8030.destroyed.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.destroyed.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$nomads.8030.intro$$nomads.8030.destroyed$\n\n$nomads.8030.anonymous$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

$nomads.8030.intro$ $nomads.8030.destroyed$\n\n$nomads.8030.anonymous$

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:774`

## nomads.8030.looted.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.looted.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$nomads.8030.intro$$nomads.8030.looted$\n\n$nomads.8030.anonymous$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:713`

### English

$nomads.8030.intro$ $nomads.8030.looted$\n\n$nomads.8030.anonymous$

## nomads.8030.stolen.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anomads.8030.stolen.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$nomads.8030.intro$现已查明，§Y[From.GetName]§!正是此次行动的幕后黑手——现在他们已经直接控制了该驿站，令其脱离了我们的驿道网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

$nomads.8030.intro$ It is now clear that the §Y[From.GetName]§! was behind this operation, as they have now assumed direct control of the waystation, effectively severing it from our wayline network.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:776`

## OUTLINER_ARKSHIP_CONSTRUCTION_AVAILABLE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOUTLINER_ARKSHIP_CONSTRUCTION_AVAILABLE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘方舟现在可以建设额外的§Y模块§!或§Y系统§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

This Arkship can support the construction of additional §YModules§! and/or §YSystems§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:779`

## OUTLINER_WAYSTATION_CONSTRUCTION_AVAILABLE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOUTLINER_WAYSTATION_CONSTRUCTION_AVAILABLE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个驿站现在可以建设额外的§Y模块§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

This Waystation can support the construction of additional §YModules§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nomads_2_l_english.yml:780`

## OUTLINER_ARKSHIP_UPGRADE_AVAILABLE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOUTLINER_ARKSHIP_UPGRADE_AVAILABLE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

该§Y方舟§!可被升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nomads_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`
